== diff(ln(x)/x^3,x)   [ok, 4 steps]
  Product rule:  diff(ln(x)/x^3, x)=(diff(ln(x), x)/x^3+ln(x)*diff(1/x^3, x))
      Known derivative:  diff(ln(x), x)=(1/x)
      Power rule:  diff(1/x^3, x)=(-3/x^4)
  = diff(ln(x)/x^3, x)=(1/(x*x^3)-3*ln(x)/x^4)
   FlowCE answer: (1-3*ln(x))/x^4
== diff(sqrt(1-x^2)*asin(x),x)   [ok, 6 steps]
  Product rule:  diff(sqrt(1-x^2)*asin(x), x)=(diff(sqrt(1-x^2), x)*asin(x)+sqrt(1-x^2)*diff(asin(x), x))
      Chain rule:  diff(sqrt(1-x^2), x)=(diff(1-x^2, x)/(2*sqrt(1-x^2)))
          Power rule:  diff(x^2, x)=(2*x)
      = diff(sqrt(1-x^2), x)=(-x/sqrt(1-x^2))
      Known derivative:  diff(asin(x), x)=(1/sqrt(-x^2+1))
  = diff(sqrt(1-x^2)*asin(x), x)=(-x*asin(x)/sqrt(1-x^2)+sqrt(1-x^2)/sqrt(-x^2+1))
   FlowCE answer: (x*sqrt(1-x^2)*asin(x)+x^2-1)/(x^2-1)
== diff(ln(x)^x,x)   [ok, 9 steps]
  Write the power with e:  ln(x)^x=(exp(x*ln(ln(x))))
  Chain rule:  diff(exp(x*ln(ln(x))), x)=(exp(x*ln(ln(x)))*diff(x*ln(ln(x)), x))
      Product rule:  diff(x*ln(ln(x)), x)=(diff(x, x)*ln(ln(x))+x*diff(ln(ln(x)), x))
          Chain rule:  diff(ln(ln(x)), x)=(diff(ln(x), x)/ln(x))
              Known derivative:  diff(ln(x), x)=(1/x)
          = diff(ln(ln(x)), x)=(1/(ln(x)*x))
      = diff(x*ln(ln(x)), x)=(ln(ln(x))+x/(ln(x)*x))
  = diff(exp(x*ln(ln(x))), x)=(exp(x*ln(ln(x)))*(ln(ln(x))+x/(ln(x)*x)))
  = diff(ln(x)^x, x)=(ln(x)^x*(ln(ln(x))+x/(ln(x)*x)))
   FlowCE answer: (ln(ln(x))+1/ln(x))*ln(x)^x
== diff(10^(5*x),x)   [ok, 4 steps]

      = diff(5*x, x)=5
  = diff(10^(5*x), x)=(5*(10^x)^5*ln(10))
   FlowCE answer: 5*ln(10)*10^x*(10^x)^4

          Power rule:  diff(1/x, x)=(-1/x^2)
          Known derivative:  diff(ln(x), x)=(1/x)
      = diff(ln(x)/x, x)=(-ln(x)/x^2+1/(x*x))
  = diff(exp(ln(x)/x), x)=(exp(ln(x)/x)*(-ln(x)/x^2+1/(x*x)))
  = diff(x^(1/x), x)=(x^(1/x)*(-ln(x)/x^2+1/(x*x)))
   FlowCE answer: (x^(1/x)-x^(1/x)*ln(x))/x^2

              Constant multiple:  diff(2*x, x)=(2*diff(x, x))
              = diff(2*x, x)=2
          = diff(exp(2*x), x)=(2*exp(2*x))
      = diff(cos(exp(2*x)), x)=(-2*sin(exp(2*x))*exp(2*x))
  = diff(ln(cos(exp(2*x))), x)=(-2*tan(exp(2*x))*exp(2*x))
   FlowCE answer: -2*exp(2*x)*sin(exp(2*x))/cos(exp(2*x))
== integrate(1/(x^2*sqrt(7-x^2)),x)   [ok, 6 steps]
  Trig substitution:  x=(sqrt(7)*sin(t)), dx=(sqrt(7)*cos(t)*dt)
  = sqrt(7-x^2)=(sqrt(7)*cos(t))
  = integrate(1/(x^2*sqrt(7-x^2)),x)=(integrate(1/(7*sin(t)^2),t))
      Known integral:  integrate(1/(7*sin(t)^2),t)=(-1/7*cot(t))
  Back to x with a right triangle:  sin(t)=(x/sqrt(7)), cos(t)=(sqrt(7-x^2)/sqrt(7))
  = integrate(1/(x^2*sqrt(7-x^2)),x)=(-sqrt(7-x^2)/(7*x))
   FlowCE answer: (x^2+sqrt(7-x^2)*sqrt(7)-7)/(7*x*(sqrt(7-x^2)-sqrt(7)))
== integrate(3/((x-2)*(x+1)),x)   [ok, 7 steps]
  Constant multiple:  integrate(3/((x-2)*(x+1)),x)=(3*integrate(1/((x-2)*(x+1)),x))
  Partial fractions:  1/((x-2)*(x+1))=(-1/(3*(x+1))+1/(3*(x-2)))
  Sum rule:  integrate(-1/(3*(x+1))+1/(3*(x-2)),x)=(-integrate(1/(3*(x+1)),x)+integrate(1/(3*(x-2)),x))
      Integral of 1/x:  integrate(1/(3*(x+1)),x)=(1/3*ln(abs(x+1)))
      Integral of 1/x:  integrate(1/(3*(x-2)),x)=(1/3*ln(abs(x-2)))
  = integrate(-1/(3*(x+1))+1/(3*(x-2)),x)=(-1/3*ln(abs(x+1))+1/3*ln(abs(x-2)))
  = integrate(3/((x-2)*(x+1)),x)=(3*(-1/3*ln(abs(x+1))+1/3*ln(abs(x-2))))
   FlowCE answer: ln(abs(x-2))-ln(abs(x+1))
== integrate(x/sqrt(2*x-x^2),x)   [ok, 14 steps]
  Split the numerator:  x/sqrt(2*x-x^2)=(-(-2*x+2)/(2*sqrt(2*x-x^2))+1/sqrt(2*x-x^2))
  Sum rule:  integrate(-(-2*x+2)/(2*sqrt(2*x-x^2))+1/sqrt(2*x-x^2),x)=(-integrate((-2*x+2)/(2*sqrt(2*x-x^2)),x)+integrate(1/sqrt(2*x-x^2),x))
      Constant multiple:  integrate((-2*x+2)/(2*sqrt(2*x-x^2)),x)=(1/2*integrate((-2*x+2)/sqrt(2*x-x^2),x))
      Substitution:  u=(2*x-x^2), du=((2-2*x)*dx)
      = integrate((-2*x+2)/sqrt(2*x-x^2),x)=(integrate(1/sqrt(u),u))
          Power rule:  integrate(1/sqrt(u),u)=(2*sqrt(u))
      Substitute back:  integrate((-2*x+2)/sqrt(2*x-x^2),x)=(2*sqrt(2*x-x^2))
      = integrate((-2*x+2)/(2*sqrt(2*x-x^2)),x)=(sqrt(2*x-x^2))
      Complete the square:  2*x-x^2=(-(x-1)^2+1)
      Substitution:  u=(x-1), du=dx
      = integrate(1/sqrt(-(x-1)^2+1),x)=(integrate(1/sqrt(-u^2+1),u))
          Arcsine formula:  integrate(1/sqrt(-u^2+1),u)=(asin(u))
      Substitute back:  integrate(1/sqrt(-(x-1)^2+1),x)=(asin(x-1))
  = integrate(-(-2*x+2)/(2*sqrt(2*x-x^2))+1/sqrt(2*x-x^2),x)=(-sqrt(2*x-x^2)+asin(x-1))
   FlowCE answer: asin(x-1)-sqrt(2*x-x^2)
== integrate(tan(2*x)^5*sec(2*x),x)   [ok, 10 steps]
  Substitution:  u=(sec(2*x)), du=(2*sin(2*x)*dx/cos(2*x)^2)
  = integrate(tan(2*x)^5*sec(2*x),x)=(integrate(1/2*(u^4-2*u^2+1),u))
      Constant multiple:  integrate(1/2*(u^4-2*u^2+1),u)=(1/2*integrate(u^4-2*u^2+1,u))
      Sum rule:  integrate(u^4-2*u^2+1,u)=(integrate(u^4,u)-integrate(2*u^2,u)+integrate(1,u))
          Power rule:  integrate(u^4,u)=(1/5*u^5)
          Power rule:  integrate(2*u^2,u)=(2/3*u^3)
          Integral of a constant:  integrate(1,u)=u
      = integrate(u^4-2*u^2+1,u)=(1/5*u^5-2/3*u^3+u)
      = integrate(1/2*(u^4-2*u^2+1),u)=(1/2*(1/5*u^5-2/3*u^3+u))
  Substitute back:  integrate(tan(2*x)^5*sec(2*x),x)=(1/2*(1/5*sec(2*x)^5-2/3*sec(2*x)^3+sec(2*x)))
   FlowCE answer: sec(2*x)^5/5/2-sec(2*x)^3/3+sec(2*x)/2
== integrate(x^2*ln(x),x)   [ok, 5 steps]
  Integration by parts:  u=(ln(x)), dv=(x^2*dx)
  = du=(dx/x), v=(1/3*x^3)
  = integrate(x^2*ln(x),x)=(1/3*x^3*ln(x)-integrate(1/3*x^2,x))
      Power rule:  integrate(1/3*x^2,x)=(1/9*x^3)
  = integrate(x^2*ln(x),x)=(1/3*x^3*ln(x)-1/9*x^3)
   FlowCE answer: (3*x^3*ln(x)-x^3)/9
== integrate(7*x^6-3*x^3+8*x^2,x)   [ok, 5 steps]

== integrate(1/(9*x^2+6*x+8),x,0,1)   [ok, 7 steps]

  Substitution:  u=(x+1/3), du=dx

== integrate(x*atan(x),x)   [ok, 9 steps]

  = du=(dx/(1+x^2)), v=(1/2*x^2)
  = integrate(x*atan(x),x)=(1/2*x^2*atan(x)-integrate(x^2/(2*x^2+2),x))
      Long division:  x^2/(2*x^2+2)=(1/2-1/(2*(x^2+1)))
      Sum rule:  integrate(1/2-1/(2*(x^2+1)),x)=(integrate(1/2,x)-integrate(1/(2*(x^2+1)),x))
          Integral of a constant:  integrate(1/2,x)=(1/2*x)
          Arctangent formula:  integrate(1/(2*(x^2+1)),x)=(1/2*atan(x))
      = integrate(1/2-1/(2*(x^2+1)),x)=(1/2*x-1/2*atan(x))
  = integrate(x*atan(x),x)=(-1/2*x+1/2*atan(x)+1/2*x^2*atan(x))

== integrate(asin(2*x),x,0,1/2)   [ok, 14 steps]
  Integration by parts:  u=(asin(2*x)), dv=dx
  = du=(2*dx/sqrt(-4*x^2+1)), v=x

== integrate(ln(x)^2,x)   [ok, 11 steps]

== integrate(exp(x)*sin(x),x)   [ok, 7 steps]

  = du=(cos(x)*dx), v=(exp(x))
  = integrate(exp(x)*sin(x),x)=(sin(x)*exp(x)-integrate(cos(x)*exp(x),x))

  Partial fractions:  x/(x^3+4*x^2+3*x+12)=(-4/(19*(x+4))+(4*x+3)/(19*(x^2+3)))
  Sum rule:  integrate(-4/(19*(x+4))+(4*x+3)/(19*(x^2+3)),x)=(integrate(-4/(19*(x+4)),x)+integrate((4*x+3)/(19*(x^2+3)),x))

  = integrate(4/(4*x^2+12*x+14),x,0,1)=(-2/5*sqrt(5)*atan(1/sqrt(5))+2/5*sqrt(5)*atan(5/(3*sqrt(5))))
   FlowCE answer: 2*(atan(sqrt(5))-atan(3/sqrt(5)))/sqrt(5)  ~ 0.196763

== integrate(x^3*cos(x),x,0,pi/2)   [ok, 19 steps]
  Integration by parts:  u=(x^3), dv=(cos(x)*dx)
  = du=(3*x^2*dx), v=(sin(x))
  = integrate(x^3*cos(x),x)=(x^3*sin(x)-integrate(3*x^2*sin(x),x))
      Constant multiple:  integrate(3*x^2*sin(x),x)=(3*integrate(x^2*sin(x),x))

          = du=dx, v=(sin(x))
          = integrate(x*cos(x),x)=(x*sin(x)-integrate(sin(x),x))
              Known integral:  integrate(sin(x),x)=(-cos(x))
          = integrate(x*cos(x),x)=(x*sin(x)+cos(x))
          = integrate(2*x*cos(x),x)=(2*(x*sin(x)+cos(x)))
      = integrate(x^2*sin(x),x)=(-x^2*cos(x)+2*(x*sin(x)+cos(x)))
      = integrate(3*x^2*sin(x),x)=(3*(-x^2*cos(x)+2*(x*sin(x)+cos(x))))
  = integrate(x^3*cos(x),x)=(x^3*sin(x)-3*(-x^2*cos(x)+2*(x*sin(x)+cos(x))))

== integrate(ln(x)/sqrt(x),x,0,1)   [ok, 12 steps]

      = dp=(2*u*du/u^2), q=u

  = integrate(ln(x)/sqrt(x),x,0,1)=-4

          Constant multiple:  integrate((u-3)/(18*u),u)=(1/18*integrate((u-3)/u,u))
          Expand:  (u-3)/u=(-3/u+1)
          Sum rule:  integrate(-3/u+1,u)=(-integrate(3/u,u)+integrate(1,u))
              Integral of 1/x:  integrate(3/u,u)=(3*ln(abs(u)))
              Integral of a constant:  integrate(1,u)=u
          = integrate(-3/u+1,u)=(-3*ln(abs(u))+u)
          = integrate((u-3)/(18*u),u)=(1/18*(-3*ln(abs(u))+u))
      Substitute back:  integrate(x^3/(3*x^2+3),x)=(1/18*(3*x^2+3-3*ln(3*x^2+3)))
  = integrate(x^2*atan(x),x)=(1/3*x^3*atan(x)-1/18*(3*x^2+3-3*ln(3*x^2+3)))
  Evaluate from a to b:  integrate(x^2*atan(x),x,0,1)=(1/3*1^3*atan(1)-1/18*(3*1^2+3-3*ln(3*1^2+3))-(1/3*0^3*atan(0)-1/18*(3*0^2+3-3*ln(3*0^2+3))))
  = integrate(x^2*atan(x),x,0,1)=(1/12*(pi+ln(4)-2))
   FlowCE answer: (pi+2*ln(2)-2)/12  ~ 0.210657

  Substitute back:  integrate(tan(3*x)^3*sec(3*x),x)=(1/3*(1/3*sec(3*x)^3-sec(3*x)))

  = integrate(tan(3*x)^3*sec(3*x),x,0,1/12*pi)=(1/9*(-sqrt(2)+2))
   FlowCE answer: (2-sqrt(2))/9  ~ 0.0650874

== integrate((x^2-6*x)/((x+5)*(x^2+8)),x)   [ok, 16 steps]

== integrate(x*atan(x),x,0,1)   [ok, 11 steps]

== integrate(sqrt(7-2*x-x^2),x)   [ok, 16 steps]

              Constant multiple:  diff(1/10*t, t)=(1/10*diff(t, t))
              = diff(1/10*t, t)=(1/10)
          = diff(exp(1/10*t), t)=(1/10*exp(1/10*t))
          Known derivative:  diff(sin(t), t)=(cos(t))
      = diff(exp(1/10*t)*sin(t), t)=(1/10*exp(1/10*t)*sin(t)+exp(1/10*t)*cos(t))
  Compute:  diff(exp(1/10*t)*cos(t), t)
      Product rule:  diff(exp(1/10*t)*cos(t), t)=(diff(exp(1/10*t), t)*cos(t)+exp(1/10*t)*diff(cos(t), t))
          Chain rule:  diff(exp(1/10*t), t)=(exp(1/10*t)*diff(1/10*t, t))
              Constant multiple:  diff(1/10*t, t)=(1/10*diff(t, t))
              = diff(1/10*t, t)=(1/10)
          = diff(exp(1/10*t), t)=(1/10*exp(1/10*t))
          Known derivative:  diff(cos(t), t)=(-sin(t))
      = diff(exp(1/10*t)*cos(t), t)=(1/10*exp(1/10*t)*cos(t)-exp(1/10*t)*sin(t))
  = (diff(exp(t/10)*sin(t), t))/(diff(exp(t/10)*cos(t), t))=((10*cos(t)+sin(t))/(cos(t)-10*sin(t)))
   FlowCE answer: (10*cos(t)+sin(t))/(cos(t)-10*sin(t))
== diff(cos(t)^3-3*cos(t),t)/diff(sin(t)^4,t)   [ok, 14 steps]
  Compute:  diff(cos(t)^3-3*cos(t), t)
      Sum rule:  diff(cos(t)^3-3*cos(t), t)=(diff(cos(t)^3, t)-(diff(3*cos(t), t)))
          Chain rule:  diff(cos(t)^3, t)=(3*cos(t)^2*diff(cos(t), t))
              Known derivative:  diff(cos(t), t)=(-sin(t))
          = diff(cos(t)^3, t)=(-3*cos(t)^2*sin(t))
          Constant multiple:  diff(3*cos(t), t)=(3*diff(cos(t), t))
              Known derivative:  diff(cos(t), t)=(-sin(t))
          = diff(3*cos(t), t)=(-3*sin(t))
      = diff(cos(t)^3-3*cos(t), t)=(-3*cos(t)^2*sin(t)+3*sin(t))
  Compute:  diff(sin(t)^4, t)
      Chain rule:  diff(sin(t)^4, t)=(4*sin(t)^3*diff(sin(t), t))
          Known derivative:  diff(sin(t), t)=(cos(t))
      = diff(sin(t)^4, t)=(4*sin(t)^3*cos(t))
  = (diff((cos(t)^3)-(3*cos(t)), t))/(diff(sin(t)^4, t))=((-3*cos(t)^2+3)/(4*cos(t)*sin(t)^2))
   FlowCE answer: 3/(4*cos(t))

      = dp=(du/u), q=u

== integrate(ln(x+1)^2,x)   [ok, 21 steps]
  Integration by parts:  u=(ln(x+1)^2), dv=dx
  = du=(2*ln(x+1)*dx/(x+1)), v=x
  = integrate(ln(x+1)^2,x)=(x*ln(x+1)^2-integrate(2*x*ln(x+1)/(x+1),x))
      Constant multiple:  integrate(2*x*ln(x+1)/(x+1),x)=(2*integrate(x*ln(x+1)/(x+1),x))
      Substitution:  u=(x+1), du=dx
      = integrate(x*ln(x+1)/(x+1),x)=(integrate((u*ln(u)-ln(u))/u,u))
          Split the numerator:  (u*ln(u)-ln(u))/u=(ln(u)-ln(u)/u)
          Sum rule:  integrate(ln(u)-ln(u)/u,u)=(integrate(ln(u),u)+integrate(-ln(u)/u,u))
              Integration by parts:  p=(ln(u)), dq=du
              = dp=(du/u), q=u
              = integrate(ln(u),u)=(u*ln(u)-integrate(1,u))
                  Integral of a constant:  integrate(1,u)=u
              = integrate(ln(u),u)=(u*ln(u)-u)
              Substitution:  w=(ln(u)), dw=(du/u)
              = integrate(ln(u)/u,u)=(integrate(w,w))
                  Power rule:  integrate(w,w)=(1/2*w^2)
              Substitute back:  integrate(ln(u)/u,u)=(1/2*ln(u)^2)
          = integrate(ln(u)-ln(u)/u,u)=(u*ln(u)-u-1/2*ln(u)^2)
      Substitute back:  integrate(x*ln(x+1)/(x+1),x)=(-x-1+(x+1)*ln(x+1)-1/2*ln(x+1)^2)
      = integrate(2*x*ln(x+1)/(x+1),x)=(2*(-x-1+(x+1)*ln(x+1)-1/2*ln(x+1)^2))
  = integrate(ln(x+1)^2,x)=(x*ln(x+1)^2-2*(-x-1+(x+1)*ln(x+1)-1/2*ln(x+1)^2))
   FlowCE answer: (x+1)*ln(x+1)^2-2*(x+1)*ln(x+1)+2*(x+1)
== integrate(x*csc(x)*cot(x),x)   [ok, 5 steps]

  = du=dx, v=(-csc(x))

  = integrate(x*csc(x)*cot(x),x)=(-x*csc(x)-ln(abs(csc(x)+cot(x))))

  Taylor polynomial:  20*x^2/(2!)-2000*x^4/(4!)=(10*x^2-250/3*x^4)
   FlowCE answer: 10*x^2-250*x^4/3+O(x^6)
== series(cos(sqrt(x)),x,0,3)   [NO STEPS, 7 steps]
   FlowCE answer: 1-x/2+x^2/24+O(x^3)
== series(cos(x)^2,x,0,6)   [ok, 14 steps]
  Derivatives:  diff(cos(x)^2, x)=(-2*sin(x)*cos(x))
  = diff(-2*sin(x)*cos(x), x)=(-2*cos(x)^2+2*sin(x)^2)
  = diff(-2*cos(x)^2+2*sin(x)^2, x)=(8*cos(x)*sin(x))
  = diff(8*cos(x)*sin(x), x)=(-8*sin(x)^2+8*cos(x)^2)
  = diff(-8*sin(x)^2+8*cos(x)^2, x)=(-32*cos(x)*sin(x))
  = diff(-32*cos(x)*sin(x), x)=(32*sin(x)^2-32*cos(x)^2)
  Their values at the center:  cos(0)^2=1
  = -2*sin(0)*cos(0)=0
  = -2*cos(0)^2+2*sin(0)^2=-2
  = 8*cos(0)*sin(0)=0
  = -8*sin(0)^2+8*cos(0)^2=8
  = -32*cos(0)*sin(0)=0
  = 32*sin(0)^2-32*cos(0)^2=-32
  Taylor polynomial:  1-2*x^2/(2!)+8*x^4/(4!)-32*x^6/(6!)=(1-x^2+1/3*x^4-2/45*x^6)
   FlowCE answer: 1-x^2+x^4/3-2*x^6/45+O(x^7)
== series(sin(x)/(1-x),x,0,3)   [ok, 8 steps]
  Derivatives:  diff(sin(x)/(1-x), x)=(((1-x)*cos(x)+sin(x))/(1-x)^2)
  = diff(((1-x)*cos(x)+sin(x))/(1-x)^2, x)=((x^2*sin(x)+2*x*cos(x)-2*x*sin(x)-2*cos(x)-sin(x))/(x^3-3*x^2+3*x-1))
  = diff((x^2*sin(x)+2*x*cos(x)-2*x*sin(x)-2*cos(x)-sin(x))/(x^3-3*x^2+3*x-1), x)=((x^3*cos(x)-3*x^2*cos(x)-3*x^2*sin(x)-3*x*cos(x)+6*x*sin(x)+5*cos(x)+3*sin(x))/(x^4-4*x^3+6*x^2-4*x+1))
  Their values at the center:  sin(0)/(1-0)=0
  = ((1-0)*cos(0)+sin(0))/(1-0)^2=1
  = (0^2*sin(0)+0*cos(0)-0*sin(0)-2*cos(0)-sin(0))/(0^3-3*0^2+0-1)=2
  = (0^3*cos(0)-3*0^2*cos(0)-3*0^2*sin(0)-0*cos(0)+0*sin(0)+5*cos(0)+3*sin(0))/(0^4-4*0^3+6*0^2-0+1)=5
  Taylor polynomial:  x+2*x^2/(2!)+5*x^3/(3!)=(x+x^2+5/6*x^3)
   FlowCE answer: x+x^2+5*x^3/6+O(x^4)
